# ESCI: GPT-6 Luna vs SID-2 high (BM25 + E5)

> **Note:** This report was generated by an LLM.

## Summary

The threshold-filtered CSV contains 377 queries; this report shows its **253 English-locale queries** only. ESCI identifies query locale through `product_locale`; this report keeps `us` and excludes other locales. The smallest included absolute NDCG difference is 0.1009; the diff script defaults to a threshold of 0.1.

- Mean NDCG for the included queries: GPT-6 Luna **0.4361**; SID-2 high **0.3900**.
- Mean NDCG difference (SID-2 minus GPT-6 Luna): **-0.0461**.
- Mean max NDCG (judgment ceiling) for these queries: **0.9600**.
- Each query section compares the top results by rank; matching rank positions do not imply matching documents.
- `NA` means the result has no explicit ESCI judgment. Grade 0 is an explicit nonrelevant label; evaluation treats unjudged results as zero relevance.
